Möbius divisor cancellation/Local checkpoint map

Exact local checkpoint receipt

Local checkpoint: original HA and independently compiled Lean verified; not Alpha-enrolled, no Alpha checked-use authority; not Stable

Actual complete proof evidence: 377 original-HA and independently compiled Lean-checked nodes. Download literal proof bundle; 2498683 bytes; SHA-256 f858f6bd9e09d6ec33b48689b385222153ad9d326eccb8239ac5776b39955542.
Exact inventory roles: 28 new owned theorems, 41 inherited prerequisites from the first 170 research theorems, 31 from the later 126, 0 current cross-track prerequisites and 276 inherited Alpha theorems. Support rows are not recounted as new. Source-helper imports are not proof premises. Alpha v30 remains 3222; Stable remains 432. This page claims complete bundle checks, not ordinary-certificate replay for every theorem. Machine-readable report.
Mathematical boundary: The input n is positive. Canonical signed code 2 denotes +1, so the sum is +1 at n=1 and zero for n>1. The positive-values theorem permits arbitrary F(0); the genuine divisor mask excludes it. Prime-square multiples contribute zero. This proves divisor cancellation, not the full Möbius inversion or convolution equivalence; full G007 remains open.
